### getBatchJobsWithChildStats()
#### Timings

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= TIMESTAMP '2025-04-30 13:49:56.725' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`

select /*+ gather_plan_statistics*/ /* 
440d3ac5-e220-4b51-ad8e-34c943cb8f2d */bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM 
(SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) 
THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN 
(cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 
succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') 
THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) 
as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN 
jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 
AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,     
 jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS
